<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A certain bottle of wine in France costs $7.5 \ \text{Euros}$. How much is 
that in English pounds?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the pounds $= a$

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
\pounds 1 : 1.2 \ \text{Euros}                  &= \pounds a : 7.5 \ \text{Euros} \\\\
\dfrac{1}{1.2}                                  &= \dfrac {a} {7.5} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {7.5} {1.2} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {75} {12} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {3 \times 25} {3 \times 4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 25} {\cancel{3} \times 4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
$

Cost $= \pounds 6.25$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \pounds                                                 &:& \text{Euros} \\\\
= & 1                                                       &:& 1.2 \\\\
= & \dfrac {1} {1.2}                                        &:& 1 \\\\
= & \dfrac {7.5} {1.2}                                      &:& 1 \times 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {75} {12}                                        &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {3 \times 25} {3 \times 4}                       &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 25} {\cancel{3} \times 4}     &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \pounds 6.25                                            &:& 7.5 \ \text{Euros}
\end{array}
$

Cost $= \pounds 6.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 6.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the pounds $= a$
<emptyline>
Using equivalent ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\pounds 1 : 1.2 \ \text{Euros}                  &= \pounds a : 7.5 \ \text{Euros} \\\\
\dfrac{1}{1.2}                                  &= \dfrac {a} {7.5} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {7.5} {1.2} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {75} {12} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {3 \times 25} {3 \times 4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 25} {\cancel{3} \times 4} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
a                                               &= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Cost $= \pounds 6.25$
</code></pre>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}

= & \dfrac {1} {1.2}                                        &:& 1 \\\\
= & \dfrac {7.5} {1.2}                                      &:& 1 \times 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {75} {12}                                        &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {3 \times 25} {3 \times 4}                       &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 25} {\cancel{3} \times 4}     &:& 7.5 \\\\
= & \pounds 6.25                                            &:& 7.5 \ \text{Euros}
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
Cost $= \pounds 6.25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question question'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>

\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of ticket}   &= \dfrac {58.10} {7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{7 \times 8.30 }{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{7} \times 8.30 }{\cancel{7}} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 8.30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 8.30$

</div>

\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of ticket}   &= \dfrac {58.10} {7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{7 \times 8.30 }{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{7} \times 8.30 }{\cancel{7}} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 8.30
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

David is $15$ stone $11$. Andrew is $275$ pounds. If there are $14$ pounds in $1$ stone what is the difference between their weights? Give your answer in pounds. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}           &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \text{ stone } 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \times 14 \text{ pounds} - 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \times 14 \text{ pounds} - 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 - 210 - 11 \\\\
                            &= 54 \text{ pounds}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$54 \text{ pounds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}           &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \text{ stone } 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \times 14 \text{ pounds} - 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 \text{ pounds} - 15 \times 14 \text{ pounds} - 11 \text{ pounds} \\\\
                            &= 275 - 210 - 11 \\\\
                            &= 54 \text{ pounds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$54 \text{ pounds}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='papername'>
<p>st-pauls-girls-school--11-plus--maths--2016--sample-paper-1:1:17</p>

<div class='rag'>
<p>Waffles Woof</p>

<div class='question question'>

In a country dance there are $3$ boys and $2$ girls in every line. 

$42$ boys take part in the dance. How many girls take part?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of girls $= g$

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \ \text{boys} : 3 \ \text{girls}              &= 42 \ \text{boys} : g \ \text{girls} \\\\
\dfrac{2}{3}                                    &= \dfrac {42} {g} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {42 \times 3} { 2} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {21 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {21 \times \cancel{2} \times 3} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
g                                               &= 63
\end{aligned}
$

Number of girls $= 63$

</div>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{boys}     &:& \text{girls} \\\\
= & 2               &:& 3  \\\\
= & 2 \times 21     &:& 3 \times 21  \\\\
= & 42              &:& 63
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$63 \ \text{girls}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of girls $= g$
<emptyline>
Using equivalent ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \ \text{boys} : 3 \ \text{girls}              &= 42 \ \text{boys} : g \ \text{girls} \\\\
\dfrac{2}{3}                                    &= \dfrac {42} {g} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {42 \times 3} { 2} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {21 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
g                                               &= \dfrac {21 \times \cancel{2} \times 3} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
g                                               &= 63
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Number of girls $= 63$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{boys}     &:& \text{girls} \\\\
= & 2               &:& 3  \\\\
= & 2 \times 21     &:& 3 \times 21  \\\\
= & 42              &:& 63
\end{array}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$63 \ \text{girls}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

There are $6$ fangos in a rango and $15$ rangos in a bango.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Alex has $9$ rangos and $4$ fangos. Barbara has $7$ rangos and $3$ fangos. How many fangos do they both have in total?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total fangos} &= \text {Alex's fangos} + \text {Barbara's fangos} \\\\
                     &= (9 \times 6 + 4) + (7 \times 6 + 3) \\\\
                     &= (54 + 4) + (42 + 3) \\\\
                     &= 58 + 45 \\\\
                     &= 103 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$103$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total fangos} &= \text {Alex's fangos} + \text {Barbara's fangos} \\\\
                     &= (9 \times 6 + 4) + (7 \times 6 + 3) \\\\
                     &= (54 + 4) + (42 + 3) \\\\
                     &= 58 + 45 \\\\
                     &= 103 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$103$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What fraction of a bango do $12$ fangos represent?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& 1 \text{rango} \\\\
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15} \ \text{bangos} \\\\
= & 6 \times 2                              &:& \dfrac {2} {15} \\\\
= & 12 \ \text{bangos}                      &:& \dfrac {2} {15} \ \text{bangos}
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2} {15}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& 1 \text{rango} \\\\
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15} \ \text{bangos} \\\\
= & 6 \times 2                              &:& \dfrac {2} {15} \\\\
= & 12 \ \text{bangos}                      &:& \dfrac {2} {15} \ \text{bangos}
\end{array}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {2} {15}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many whole bangos are there in $274$ fangos? How many fangos are left over?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& 1 \text{rango} \\\\
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15} \ \text{bangos} \\\\
= & 1                                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15 \times 6} \\\\
= & 274                                     &:& \dfrac {274} {15 \times 6} \\\\
= & 274                                     &:& \dfrac {274} {90} \\\\
= & 274 \ \text{fangos}                     &:& 3 \dfrac {4} {90}  \ \text{bangos}
\end{array}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bangos}         &= 3 \\\\
\text{Left over bangos}     &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3, 4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using ratio,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& 1 \text{rango} \\\\
= & 6 \ \text{fangos}                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15} \ \text{bangos} \\\\
= & 1                                       &:& \dfrac {1} {15 \times 6} \\\\
= & 274                                     &:& \dfrac {274} {15 \times 6} \\\\
= & 274                                     &:& \dfrac {274} {90} \\\\
= & 274 \ \text{fangos}                     &:& 3 \dfrac {4} {90}  \ \text{bangos}
\end{array}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total bangos}         &= 3 \\\\
\text{Left over bangos}     &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3, 4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

part (b): remove whole katex

1 bango    = 15 rangos

1 bango    = 15 x 6 fangos

1 bango    = 90 fangos

1/90 bango = 1 fangos

Fraction   = 12/90

and solve further

part (c) :

remove whole katex:

\text {Bangos} &= 274/90 \\\\

               &= 3 \ \text {remainder} \ 4 \\\\

               &\approx 3

<div class='question question'>

How many bags of crisps at $16 \text{p}$ each can I buy for $\pounds 2$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of crisp bags}    &= \dfrac {\text {Total money}} {\text {Money per bag}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\pounds 2} {16 \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {200} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25 \times 8} {2 \times 8} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25 \times \cancel{8}} {2 \times \cancel{8}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25} {2} \\\\
                                &= 12 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &= 12
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of crisp bags}    &= \dfrac {\text {Total money}} {\text {Money per bag}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\pounds 2} {16 \text{p}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {200} {16} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25 \times 8} {2 \times 8} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25 \times \cancel{8}} {2 \times \cancel{8}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {25} {2} \\\\
                                &= 12 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many Gammas in $5$ Alphas, $6$ Betas and $3$ Gammas?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \ \text{Alphas} + 6 \ \text{Betas} + 3 \ \text{gammas} \\\\
&= 5 \times 20 \times 5 \ \text{Gammas} + 6 \times 3 \ \text{Gammas} + 3 \ \text{gammas} \\\\
&= 500 + 18 + 3 \\\\
&= 521 \ \text{Gammas}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$521 \ \text{Gammas}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \ \text{Alphas} + 6 \ \text{Betas} + 3 \ \text{gammas} \\\\
&= 5 \times 20 \times 5 \ \text{Gammas} + 6 \times 3 \ \text{Gammas} + 3 \ \text{gammas} \\\\
&= 500 + 18 + 3 \\\\
&= 521 \ \text{Gammas}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$521 \ \text{Gammas}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Using as many alphas as you can, and then betas, then gammas, how would you pay for something that costs $789$ Gammas?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of alphas}     &= \dfrac {789} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {789} {100} \\\\
                            &= 7 \dfrac {89} {100} \\\\
                            &= 7 \\\\
\text{Remaining gammas}     &= 789 - 7 \times 5 \times 20 \\\\
                            &= 789 - 700 \\\\
                            &= 89 \\\\
\text{Number of betas}      &= \dfrac {89} {5} \\\\
                            &= 17 \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
                            &= 17 \\\\
\text{Remaining gammas}     &= 89 - 17 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 89 - 85 \\\\
                            &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7 \ \text{Alphas} + 17 \ \text{Betas} + 4 \ \text{Gammas}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of alphas}     &= \dfrac {789} {20 \times 5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {789} {100} \\\\
                            &= 7 \dfrac {89} {100} \\\\
                            &= 7 \\\\
\text{Remaining gammas}     &= 789 - 7 \times 5 \times 20 \\\\
                            &= 789 - 700 \\\\
                            &= 89 \\\\
\text{Number of betas}      &= \dfrac {89} {5} \\\\
                            &= 17 \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
                            &= 17 \\\\
\text{Remaining gammas}     &= 89 - 17 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 89 - 85 \\\\
                            &= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find the total number of feet in $3 \ \text{furlongs}, 2 \ \text{chains}$ and $5 \ \text{yards}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \ \text{furlongs} + 2 \ \text{chains} + 5 \ \text{yards} \\\\
&= 3 \times 10 \times 22 \times 3 \ \text{feet} + 2 \times 22 \times 3 \ \text{feet} + 5 \times 3 \ \text{feet} \\\\
&= 1980 + 132 + 15 \\\\
&= 2127 \ \text{feet}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2127 \ \text{feet}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \ \text{furlongs} + 2 \ \text{chains} + 5 \ \text{yards} \\\\
&= 3 \times 10 \times 22 \times 3 \ \text{feet} + 2 \times 22 \times 3 \ \text{feet} + 5 \times 3 \ \text{feet} \\\\
&= 1980 + 132 + 15 \\\\
&= 2127 \ \text{feet}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2127 \ \text{feet}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Convert $472 \ \text{feet}$ to chains, yards and feet using as many chains as possible, then yards and then feet.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of chains}     &= \dfrac {472} {22 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {472} {66} \\\\
                            &= 7 \dfrac {10} {66} \\\\
                            &= 7 \\\\
\text{Remaining feet}       &= 472 - 7 \times 22 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 472 - 462 \\\\
                            &= 10 \\\\
\text{Number of yards}      &= \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
                            &= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                            &= 3 \\\\
\text{Remaining feet}       &= 10 - 3 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 10 - 9 \\\\
                            &= 1
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7 \ \text{chains} + 3 \ \text{yards} + 1 \ \text{foot}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of chains}     &= \dfrac {472} {22 \times 3} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {472} {66} \\\\
                            &= 7 \dfrac {10} {66} \\\\
                            &= 7 \\\\
\text{Remaining feet}       &= 472 - 7 \times 22 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 472 - 462 \\\\
                            &= 10 \\\\
\text{Number of yards}      &= \dfrac {10} {3} \\\\
                            &= 3 \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
                            &= 3 \\\\
\text{Remaining feet}       &= 10 - 3 \times 3 \\\\
                            &= 10 - 9 \\\\
                            &= 1
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7 \ \text{chains} + 3 \ \text{yards} + 1 \ \text{foot}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>
